RICO KNOWLEDGE MODULE
SOURCE MODULE: 08_PSO_SECTION_ID_STRATEGY_OVERLAP.txt
MOVE ROUTING: See 18_RICO_KNOWLEDGE_INDEX_QUERY_MAP.txt

PSO SECTION ID STRATEGY, COMMUNITY LAYER, AND OVERLAP
=====================================================

PURPOSE
Computed Section-ID database summaries, pair overlap, and a separate community strategy layer. Database coverage and community rankings must not be conflated.

SOURCE BASIS
section-id-strategy.json; section-id-overlap.json; community-section-id.json.

RETRIEVAL RULE
Treat explicit platform labels, verification/status fields, source labels, and warnings as part of the fact. Never silently remove them.
